{"forms":[{"form":"trigonometries","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Trigonometry emerged in the Hellenistic world during the 3rd century BCE from applications of geometry to astronomy; the Greeks focused on the calculation of chords, while mathematicians in India created the earliest known tables of values for trigonometric functions such as sine.","type":"example"},{"text":"Historically, trigonometry has been applied in areas such as geodesy, surveying, celestial mechanics and navigation.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The branch of mathematics that deals with the relationships between the sides and angles of (in particular) right-angled triangles, as represented by the trigonometric functions, and with calculations based on said relationships."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["geometry","mathematical-analysis","mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌtɹɪɡəˈnɒmətɹi/"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"branch of mathematics","tags":["abbreviation","informal"],"word":"trig"}],"upstream_line":123309,"upstream_offset":580219039,"upstream_record_sha256":"48d56538e9fc5730a278e496302be461122e9fe9721d1f5c8804fc6e89fe0d4d","word":"trigonometry"}
